You are given a 9x9 Sudoku puzzle as `input.png` in your working directory.

Your task:


1. Read the puzzle from `input.png` (identify the given clue digits and their positions).
2. Solve the puzzle. Standard Sudoku rules apply: every row, every column, and
   every 3x3 box must contain the digits 1-9 exactly once.
3. Save your completed solution as `output.png` in the working directory.


Constraints on the output image:


- `output.png` MUST have the same dimensions (width, height) as `input.png`.
- `output.png` MUST use the same 9x9 grid layout and cell coordinates as `input.png`.
- The original clue digits must remain in their original positions.
- Every empty cell in the input must be filled with the correct digit in the output.
- Each digit should be centered inside its cell in a clear, readable font.


Code constraints:
- YOU ARE ALLOWED TO WRITE CODE/SHELL TO READ THE INPUT IMAGE (BUT ONLY IF THE CODE CAN BE GENERALIZED FOR ANY IMAGE NOT JUST SUDOKU)
- YOU ARE NOT ALLOWED TO WRITE CODE TO SOLVE THE SUDOKU.
- YOU ARE ALLOWED TO WRITE CODE/SHELL TO CREATE THE SOLUTION IMAGE OUTPUT.PNG


DEBUGGING:
- If a tool is not available, please log that the tool is not available and continue without.
This allows the user to update the harness to include the tool in future runs.


End by ensuring `output.png` exists in the working directory.

If lines are ~3px wide, gap = 64-3-... hmm lines at 16 and 80: line spans maybe 15-18 and 79-82? Then gap = 19..78 = 60px. But glyph region analysis used lines[i]+5..lines[i+1]-5 = 21..74 = 54px. The white gap is bigger than that, so blanking lines+5..-5 leaves the margins' white anyway (they're white already). So blanking lines[c]+5..lines[c+1]-5 then drawing digit is safe and won't touch the grid lines.
So:

Copy input to out.
For each cell, fill rect (x0=lines[c]+5, y0=lines[r]+5, x1=lines[c+1]-5, y1=lines[r+1]-5) with white (255,255,255). (PIL uses (x0,y0,x1,y1) inclusive-exclusive: lines[c]+5 to lines[c+1]-5 exclusive → fills up to lines[c+1]-6; leaves 1px white gap. Fine.)
Draw digit centered: render text with ImageFont at size 44, get bbox via font.getbbox or draw.textbbox, center within content area (54x54 region).
